    17	- No SIGKILL, no subprocess timeouts, no ulimit, no deletion or overwrite, no reading of old FIFOs.

   630	def segments(path):
   631	    with open(path, "rb") as f:
   632	        txt = f.read().decode("utf-8", "replace")
   633	    return [s for s in re.split(r"[\r\n]", txt) if s != ""]
   634	
   635	
   636	def supervise(child, sink):
   637	    """Monitor until exit or a single trip; any monitor exception is itself a trip."""
   638	    me = os.getpid()
   639	    last_rss = last_reg = 0.0
   640	    peak = 0
   641	    while child.alive():
   642	        try:
   643	            now = time.time()
   644	            if CANCEL.is_set():
   645	                child.term("owner_cancel_sigterm")
   646	            elif now > child.deadline:
   647	                child.term("absolute_or_wall_deadline")
   648	            elif sink is not None and sink.over_cap:
   649	                child.term("proof_cap")
   650	            elif sink is not None and sink.error:
   651	                child.term("proof_sink_error")
   652	            elif child.over_cap:
   653	                child.term("capture_cap")
   654	            elif free_bytes() < FREE_FLOOR:
   655	                child.term("inflight_disk_floor")
   656	            if child.term_sent_at is None and now - last_rss >= 1.0:
   657	                last_rss = now
   658	                found, rss = child.census()
   659	                rows = ps_table()
   660	                runner_rss = sum(r[3] for r in rows if r[0] == me) * 1024
   661	                if found:
   662	                    child.descendants.extend(found)
   663	                    child.term("descendant_process")
   664	                total = rss + runner_rss
   665	                peak = max(peak, total)
   666	                if runner_rss == 0 or (rss == 0 and child.alive()):
   667	                    child.term("rss_unreadable")
   668	                elif total > LANE_RSS_TRIP:
   669	                    child.term("lane_rss")
   670	            if child.term_sent_at is None and now - last_reg >= 5.0:
   671	                last_reg = now
   672	                if registry_refusals():
   673	                    child.term("stop_registry")
   674	        except Exception as exc:
   675	            child.term("monitor_exception:%r" % exc)
   676	        if child.term_sent_at is not None:
   677	            break
   678	        time.sleep(0.25)
   679	    if child.stop is not None and child.stop not in NONLATCHING_TRIPS:
   680	        latch("trip %s on %s" % (child.stop, child.label))
   681	    return child.stop, peak
   682	
   683	
   684	def run_child(argv, label, wall, reserve, inputs, sink=None):
   685	    """Prepared outputs -> admission -> spawn -> supervise -> settle.  Returns (rec|None, lines, refusal)."""
   686	    ch = None
   687	    try:
   688	        ch = Child(argv, label, wall, reserve, inputs, sink)
   689	        if ch.refused:
   690	            return None, [], ch.refused
   691	        peak = None
   692	        try:
   693	            if CANCEL.is_set():
   694	                ch.term("owner_cancel_sigterm")
   695	            _, peak = supervise(ch, sink)
   696	        except BaseException as exc:
   697	            latch("runner exception during supervision %r" % exc)
   698	            ch.term("runner_exception")
   699	        rec = ch.settle()
   700	        rec["trip"] = ch.stop
   701	        rec["peak_sampled_lane_rss_bytes"] = peak
   702	        lines = segments(ch.out_path) if rec.get("stream_thread_alive_after_join") is False else []
   703	        return rec, lines, []
   704	    finally:
   705	        if ch is not None and getattr(ch, "proc", None) is not None and ch.settled_rec is None:
   706	            ch.settle()  # any escape above still reaps and settles the owned child
   707	        if sink is not None and (ch is None or getattr(ch, "proc", None) is None):
   708	            sink.finish()  # never-spawned producer: unblock and join the reader
   709	
   710	
   711	def run_kissat(cnf_path, proof_file, label, seconds, cap, reserve, inputs):
   712	    argv = [KISSAT[0], "-q", "-f", "--seed=1", "--time=%d" % seconds, cnf_path]
   713	    sink = None
   714	    if proof_file is not None:
   715	        fifo_dir = os.path.join(STATE["out"], "fifo")
   716	        os.makedirs(fifo_dir, exist_ok=True)
   717	        sink = ProofSink(os.path.join(fifo_dir, label + ".proof.fifo"), proof_file, cap)
   718	        argv.append(sink.fifo_path)
   719	    return run_child(argv, label + ".kissat", seconds, reserve, dict(inputs, proof_file=proof_file, proof_cap=cap), sink)
   720	
   721	
   722	def run_drat(cnf_path, proof_path, label, seconds, reserve, inputs):
   723	    return run_child([DRAT, cnf_path, proof_path, "-i", "-t", str(seconds)], label + ".drat", seconds, reserve,
   724	                     dict(inputs, cnf=cnf_path, proof=proof_path))

   726	
   727	def sink_complete(sink_rec):
   728	    return (sink_rec is not None and sink_rec.get("sink_settled") and sink_rec["connected"] and sink_rec["eof"]
   729	            and not sink_rec["over_cap"] and sink_rec["error"] is None and not sink_rec["unblocked_by_runner"]
   730	            and sink_rec["discarded"] == 0 and sink_rec["received"] == sink_rec["written"]
   731	            and sink_rec.get("file_rehash") == [sink_rec["sha256_of_written"], sink_rec["written"]])
   732	
   733	
   734	def qualify_unsat(cnf_expect, solver_rec, kissat_lines, proof_sha, proof_bytes, proof_cap, drat_rec, drat_lines,
   735	                  cnf_post, proof_post):
   736	    bad = []
   737	    if solver_rec["returncode"] != 20 or solver_rec["stop"] != "self":
   738	        bad.append("solver not self-exit rc20")
   739	    if [s for s in kissat_lines if s.startswith("s ")] != ["s UNSATISFIABLE"]:
   740	        bad.append("solver status lines not exactly ['s UNSATISFIABLE']")
   741	    if not sink_complete(solver_rec.get("proof_sink")):
   742	        bad.append("proof sink incomplete/over cap/error")
   743	    if proof_bytes <= 0 or proof_bytes > proof_cap:
   744	        bad.append("proof size outside (0, cap]")
   745	    if drat_rec["returncode"] != 0 or drat_rec["stop"] != "self":
   746	        bad.append("checker not self-exit rc0")
   747	    st = [s for s in drat_lines if s.startswith("s ")]
   748	    if st != ["s VERIFIED"]:
   749	        bad.append("checker status lines %r" % st)
   750	    v, c = cnf_expect[2].split()[2:4]
   751	    if ("c parsing input formula with %s variables and %s clauses" % (v, c)) not in drat_lines:
   752	        bad.append("checker parse header line missing/mismatched")
   753	    if ("c finished parsing, read %d bytes from proof file" % proof_bytes) not in drat_lines:
   754	        bad.append("checker-consumed proof bytes != proof file bytes")
   755	    for pat in ("early EOF", "clauses less than expected", "trivial UNSAT", "ERROR", "MEMOUT",
   756	                "wrong binary prefix", "no empty clause detected", "NOT VERIFIED", "TIMEOUT"):
   757	        if any(pat in s for s in drat_lines):
   758	            bad.append("checker output contains %r" % pat)
   759	    if cnf_post != (cnf_expect[0], cnf_expect[1]):
   760	        bad.append("CNF file digest/bytes != expected")
   761	    if proof_post != (proof_sha, proof_bytes):
   762	        bad.append("proof file digest/bytes != sink measurement")
   763	    for rec in (solver_rec, drat_rec):
   764	        if not rec["settled"] or rec["capture_over_cap"]:
   765	            bad.append("settlement/capture defect seq %s" % rec["seq"])
   766	    return bad
   767	
   768	
   769	def verify_sat(cnf_path, kissat_lines, label):
   770	    """Two independent checks of a SAT model: clause satisfaction and both PA19 definitions."""
   771	    lits = set()
   772	    for s in kissat_lines:
   773	        if s.startswith("v "):
   774	            lits.update(int(x) for x in s[2:].split() if x != "0")
   775	    sat_all = True
   776	    with open(cnf_path, "rb") as f:
   777	        for ln in f:
   778	            if ln.startswith(b"p") or ln.startswith(b"c"):
   779	                continue
   780	            cl = [int(x) for x in ln.split()[:-1]]
   781	            if not any(l in lits for l in cl):
   782	                sat_all = False
   783	                break
   784	    A = [[None] * 8 for _ in range(19)]
   785	    cells_ok = True
   786	    for r in range(19):
   787	        for c in range(8):
   788	            ss = [s for s in range(6) if (1 + 48 * r + 6 * c + s) in lits]
   789	            if len(ss) != 1:
   790	                cells_ok = False
   791	            else:
   792	                A[r][c] = ss[0]
   793	    rowpair_ok = cells_ok and all(sum(A[r][c] == A[q][c] for c in range(8)) <= 1
   794	                                  for r in range(19) for q in range(r + 1, 19))
   795	    colpair_ok = cells_ok and all(len({(A[r][c], A[r][d]) for r in range(19)}) == 19
   796	                                  for c in range(8) for d in range(c + 1, 8))
   797	    cand = os.path.join(STATE["out"], label + ".SAT-CANDIDATE.json")
   798	    write_x(cand, {"host_clock": clock(), "array": A, "clauses_all_satisfied": sat_all,
   799	                   "cells_exactly_one": cells_ok, "row_pair_agreements_le_1": rowpair_ok,
   800	                   "column_pair_ordered_pairs_distinct": colpair_ok,
   801	                   "status": "CANDIDATE_UNVERIFIED - notify parent immediately"})
   802	    latch_path = os.path.join(LANE, "control", "SAT-CANDIDATE-" + label)
   803	    write_bytes_x(latch_path, cand.encode())
   804	    return {"candidate": cand, "latch": latch_path, "clauses_all_satisfied": sat_all, "cells": cells_ok,
   805	            "rowpair": rowpair_ok, "colpair": colpair_ok}
   806	
   807	
   808	def php_cnf(p, h):
   809	    """Pigeonhole: p pigeons into h holes; UNSAT iff p > h."""
   810	    var = lambda i, j: i * h + j + 1  # noqa: E731
   811	    cl = [[var(i, j) for j in range(h)] for i in range(p)]
   812	    for j in range(h):
   813	        for i in range(p):
   814	            for k in range(i + 1, p):
   815	                cl.append([-var(i, j), -var(k, j)])
   816	    return ("p cnf %d %d\n" % (p * h, len(cl)) + "".join(" ".join(map(str, c)) + " 0\n" for c in cl)).encode()
   817	
   818	
   819	def healthy(rec):
   820	    """A control child must have exited by itself (rc>=0), with no trip, fully settled."""
   821	    return rec is not None and rec["stop"] == "self" and rec["settled"] and not rec["capture_over_cap"] \
   822	        and rec["returncode"] is not None and rec["returncode"] >= 0
   823	
   824	
   825	def controls():
   826	    """Same-binary positive and intended-reason mutation controls; stops at the first refused,
   827	    unhealthy or not-as-intended control (later controls are not started)."""
   828	    recs = {}
   829	    d = os.path.join(STATE["out"], "controls")
   830	    os.mkdir(d)
   831	    per_child = LOG_CAP + CONTROL_PROOF_CAP + RECORD_ALLOW // 8
   832	
   833	    def fail(name, why):
   834	        recs[name] = dict(recs.get(name, {}), failed=why)
   835	        return False, recs
   836	
   837	    def pin(path):
   838	        return list(sha_file(path))
   839	
   840	    raw = php_cnf(6, 5)
   841	    cnf = os.path.join(d, "php6x5.cnf")
   842	    h, n = write_bytes_x(cnf, raw)
   843	    exp = (h, n, raw.split(b"\n", 1)[0].decode())
   844	    proof = os.path.join(d, "php6x5.drat")
   845	    ks, kl, rf = run_kissat(cnf, proof, "C1", CONTROL_CHILD_WALL, CONTROL_PROOF_CAP, per_child, {"cnf_pin": [h, n]})
   846	    if ks is None:
   847	        return fail("C1_positive_php6x5", "refused %r" % rf)
   848	    recs["C1_positive_php6x5"] = {"kissat": ks}
   849	    if not (healthy(ks) and sink_complete(ks["proof_sink"])):
   850	        return fail("C1_positive_php6x5", "solver leg unhealthy or sink incomplete")
   851	    ph, pn = sha_file(proof)
   852	    dr, dl, rf = run_drat(cnf, proof, "C1", CONTROL_CHILD_WALL, per_child, {"cnf_pin": [h, n], "proof_pin": [ph, pn]})
   853	    if dr is None:
   854	        return fail("C1_positive_php6x5", "checker refused %r" % rf)
   855	    c1 = qualify_unsat(exp, ks, kl, ph, pn, CONTROL_PROOF_CAP, dr, dl, sha_file(cnf), sha_file(proof))
   856	    recs["C1_positive_php6x5"].update(intended="PASS", defects=c1, drat=dr)
   857	    if c1 != [] or not healthy(dr):
   858	        return fail("C1_positive_php6x5", "positive control did not pass")
   859	    # C2 (deterministic): well-formed CNF, same header and clause count, first clause missing one literal.
   860	    lines = raw.split(b"\n")
   861	    first = lines[1].split()
   862	    mutated = b"\n".join([lines[0], b" ".join(first[:-2] + first[-1:])] + lines[2:])
   863	    cnf2 = os.path.join(d, "php6x5-one-literal-removed.cnf")
   864	    h2, n2 = write_bytes_x(cnf2, mutated)
   865	    dr2, dl2, rf = run_drat(cnf2, proof, "C2", CONTROL_CHILD_WALL, per_child, {"cnf_pin": [h2, n2], "proof_pin": [ph, pn]})
   866	    if dr2 is None:
   867	        return fail("C2_mutated_cnf_digest", "refused %r" % rf)
   868	    c2 = qualify_unsat(exp, ks, kl, ph, pn, CONTROL_PROOF_CAP, dr2, dl2, sha_file(cnf2), (ph, pn))
   869	    recs["C2_mutated_cnf_digest"] = {"intended": "REJECT with 'CNF file digest/bytes != expected'; healthy checker",
   870	                                     "defects": c2, "drat": dr2,
   871	                                     "checker_status_lines": [s for s in dl2 if s.startswith("s ")]}
   872	    if not (healthy(dr2) and "CNF file digest/bytes != expected" in c2):
   873	        return fail("C2_mutated_cnf_digest", "intended reason or health missing")
   874	    # C3: valid proof + one trailing garbage byte: consumed bytes != file bytes and 'wrong binary prefix'.
   875	    with open(proof, "rb") as f:
   876	        pbytes = f.read()
   877	    proof3 = os.path.join(d, "php6x5-trailing.drat")
   878	    p3h, p3n = write_bytes_x(proof3, pbytes + b"x")
   879	    dr3, dl3, rf = run_drat(cnf, proof3, "C3", CONTROL_CHILD_WALL, per_child, {"cnf_pin": [h, n], "proof_pin": [p3h, p3n]})
   880	    if dr3 is None:
   881	        return fail("C3_trailing_proof_byte", "refused %r" % rf)

   142	        STATE["cancel_order"] = next(ORDER)  # atomic w.r.t. the main thread's bytecode boundaries

   150	def clock():
   151	    return subprocess.run(["/bin/date", "+%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z"], capture_output=True, text=True,
   152	                          check=True, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL).stdout.strip()

   155	def sha_file(path):
   156	    st = os.lstat(path)
   157	    if not stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode):
   158	        raise RuntimeError("not a regular file: " + path)
   159	    h = hashlib.sha256()
   160	    n = 0
   161	    with open(path, "rb") as f:
   162	        for b in iter(lambda: f.read(1 << 20), b""):
   163	            h.update(b)
   164	            n += len(b)
   165	    return h.hexdigest(), n
   166	
   167	
   168	def read_pinned(path, expect):
   169	    st = os.lstat(path)
   170	    if not stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode):
   171	        raise RuntimeError("not a regular file: " + path)
   172	    with open(path, "rb") as f:
   173	        raw = f.read()
   174	    got = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
   175	    if got != expect:
   176	        raise RuntimeError(f"pin mismatch {path}: {got} != {expect}")
   177	    return raw
   178	
   179	
   180	def write_x(path, obj):
   181	    data = json.dumps(obj, indent=1, sort_keys=True).encode()
   182	    with open(path, "xb") as f:
   183	        f.write(data)
   184	        f.flush()
   185	        os.fsync(f.fileno())
   186	    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
   187	
   188	
   189	def write_bytes_x(path, data):
   190	    with open(path, "xb") as f:
   191	        f.write(data)
   192	        f.flush()
   193	        os.fsync(f.fileno())
   194	    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), len(data)
   195

   197	def free_bytes():
   198	    st = os.statvfs(LANE)
   199	    return st.f_bavail * st.f_frsize

   202	def ps_table():
   203	    """Process table rows (pid, ppid, pgid, rss_kib); raises on failure (callers latch)."""
   204	    out = subprocess.run(["/bin/ps", "-A", "-o", "pid=,ppid=,pgid=,rss="], capture_output=True, text=True,
   205	                         stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL)
   206	    if out.returncode != 0:
   207	        raise RuntimeError("ps -A failed rc %d" % out.returncode)
   208	    rows = []
   209	    for ln in out.stdout.splitlines():
   210	        parts = ln.split()
   211	        if len(parts) != 4 or not all(p.isdigit() for p in parts):
   212	            raise RuntimeError("malformed ps row %r" % ln)
   213	        rows.append(tuple(int(p) for p in parts))
   214	    if not rows:
   215	        raise RuntimeError("empty ps table")
   216	    return rows
   217	
   218	
   219	def identity_checked(pid, runner_pid):
   220	    """Start identity; must parse and show ppid==runner and pgid==pid (own session)."""
   221	    out = subprocess.run(["/bin/ps", "-o", "pid=,ppid=,pgid=,lstart=,comm=", "-p", str(pid)],
   222	                         capture_output=True, text=True, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL)
   223	    line = out.stdout.strip()
   224	    parts = line.split()
   225	    if out.returncode != 0 or len(parts) < 8 or not all(p.isdigit() for p in parts[:3]):
   226	        raise RuntimeError("unavailable/malformed start identity %r" % line)
   227	    if int(parts[0]) != pid or int(parts[1]) != runner_pid or int(parts[2]) != pid:
   228	        raise RuntimeError("start identity mismatch %r" % line)
   229	    return line

   232	def registry_refusals():
   233	    """Fail-closed STOP registry: new-round STOP*/HALT*/SAT-CANDIDATE* markers in LANE/control or
   234	    RUN/control; any change, loss or addition in the inherited old cycle2-root STOP/HALT set."""
   235	    why = []
   236	    for d in (LANE + "/control", RUN + "/control"):
   237	        for name in sorted(os.listdir(d)):
   238	            if re.match(r"(?i)^(stop|halt|sat-candidate)", name) or "HALT" in name:
   239	                why.append("new-round marker " + os.path.join(d, name))
   240	    c2 = OLD + "/cycle2"
   241	    present = sorted(n for n in os.listdir(c2) if re.match(r"^(STOP|HALT)", n) or "HALT" in n)
   242	    if present != sorted(OLD_LIVE_MARKERS):
   243	        why.append("inherited cycle2 marker set changed: " + json.dumps(present))
   244	    for name, h in OLD_LIVE_MARKERS.items():
   245	        try:
   246	            if sha_file(os.path.join(c2, name))[0] != h:
   247	                why.append("inherited marker changed: " + name)
   248	        except Exception as exc:
   249	            why.append(f"inherited marker unreadable: {name}: {exc!r}")
   250	    try:
   251	        if sha_file(REGISTRY[0])[0] != REGISTRY[1]:
   252	            why.append("lane registry changed")
   253	        if sha_file(LAUNCH_CONTRACT[0])[0] != LAUNCH_CONTRACT[1]:
   254	            why.append("launch contract changed")
   255	    except Exception as exc:
   256	        why.append("registry/contract unreadable: %r" % exc)
   257	    return why

   260	def _aware(s):
   261	    dt = datetime.fromisoformat(s)
   262	    if dt.tzinfo is None:
   263	        raise ValueError("naive timestamp")
   264	    return dt

   267	def _int(x, lo=None):
   268	    if not isinstance(x, int) or isinstance(x, bool) or (lo is not None and x < lo):
   269	        raise ValueError("bad integer %r" % (x,))
   270	    return x

   922	    st6 = [s for s in kl6 if s.startswith("s ")]
   923	    recs["C6_native_time_limit"] = {"intended": "healthy self-exit, rc not 10/20, no SAT/UNSAT status line",
   924	                                    "kissat": ks6, "status_lines": st6}
   925	    if not (healthy(ks6) and ks6["returncode"] not in (10, 20)
   926	            and not any(s in ("s SATISFIABLE", "s UNSATISFIABLE") for s in st6)):
   927	        return fail("C6_native_time_limit", "intended reason or health missing")
   928	    # C7: missing input with a proof sink: kissat self-exit rc>0 naming the path; sink settles.
   929	    absent = os.path.join(d, "absent.cnf")
   930	    ks7, kl7, rf = run_kissat(absent, os.path.join(d, "absent.drat"), "C7", 10, CONTROL_PROOF_CAP, per_child,
   931	                              {"cnf_pin": None})
   932	    if ks7 is None:
   933	        return fail("C7_no_input_sink_liveness", "refused %r" % rf)
   934	    recs["C7_no_input_sink_liveness"] = {"intended": "healthy self-exit rc>0 not 10/20 mentioning absent.cnf; sink joined",
   935	                                         "kissat": ks7}
   936	    if not (healthy(ks7) and ks7["returncode"] not in (0, 10, 20) and any("absent.cnf" in s for s in kl7)
   937	            and not ks7["proof_sink"]["thread_alive_after_join"]):
   938	        return fail("C7_no_input_sink_liveness", "intended reason or settlement missing")
   939	    return True, recs
   940	
   941	
   942	def terminal_gate():
   943	    """Registry and disk evidence collected BEFORE the commit boundary; any failure latches (r2#4)."""
   944	    why = []
   945	    try:
   946	        why += registry_refusals()
   947	        if free_bytes() < FREE_FLOOR:
   948	            why.append("free below in-flight floor at terminal gate")
   949	    except Exception as exc:
   950	        why.append("terminal gate exception %r" % exc)
   951	    for w in why:
   952	        latch("terminal gate: " + w)
   953	    return why
   954	
   955	
   956	def commit_record(path, rec_unknown, rec_unsat):
   957	    """r2#1: explicit commit boundary.  Both serializations and the O_EXCL fd exist before the boundary;
   958	    the boundary is one atomic next(ORDER); a cancel stamped before it forces UNKNOWN."""
   959	    unknown_bytes = json.dumps(rec_unknown, indent=1, sort_keys=True).encode()
   960	    unsat_bytes = None if rec_unsat is None else json.dumps(rec_unsat, indent=1, sort_keys=True).encode()
   961	    fd = os.open(path, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL, 0o644)
   962	    try:
   963	        boundary = next(ORDER)
   964	        co = STATE["cancel_order"]
   965	        chosen = unsat_bytes if (unsat_bytes is not None and not LATCH and (co is None or co > boundary)) else unknown_bytes
   966	        view = memoryview(chosen)
   967	        while view:
   968	            k = os.write(fd, view)
   969	            view = view[k:]
   970	        os.fsync(fd)
   971	    finally:
   972	        os.close(fd)
   973	    return {"boundary": boundary, "cancel_order": STATE["cancel_order"], "committed_unsat": chosen is unsat_bytes,
   974	            "sha256": hashlib.sha256(chosen).hexdigest()}
   975	
   976	
   977	def main():
   978	    signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, on_term)
   979	    signal.signal(signal.SIGINT, on_term)
   980	    if len(sys.argv) != 4:
   981	        print("usage: pilot_file_drat_v9.py GO.json GO_SHA256 OUT_DIR", flush=True)
   982	        return EXIT_PRESTART_REFUSED
   983	    go_path, go_sha, out_dir = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], os.path.abspath(sys.argv[3])
   984	    if not out_dir.startswith(LANE + "/evidence/pilot-v1/"):
   985	        print("REFUSED: output outside LANE/evidence/pilot-v1/", flush=True)
   986	        return EXIT_PRESTART_REFUSED
   987	    self_sha = sha_file(os.path.abspath(__file__))[0]
   988	    try:
   989	        go = json.loads(read_pinned(go_path, go_sha))
   990	        read_pinned(*BINARY_IMPORTS)
   991	    except Exception as exc:
   992	        print("REFUSED: GO/evidence unreadable or pin mismatch %r" % exc, flush=True)
   993	        return EXIT_PRESTART_REFUSED
   994	    if go.get("runner_sha256") != self_sha or go.get("decision") != "GO" \
   995	            or go.get("items") != [it["id"] for it in ITEMS]:
   996	        print("REFUSED: GO record does not name this runner/items", flush=True)
   997	        return EXIT_PRESTART_REFUSED
   998	    t_start = time.time()
   999	    abs_end = t_start + CONTROLS_WORST_SECONDS + len(ITEMS) * ITEM_WORST_SECONDS
  1000	    if abs_end + SETTLE + MARGIN > t_start + MAX_CHUNK or abs_end > OWNED_SETTLED_BY - 180:
  1001	        print("REFUSED: chunk-fit", flush=True)
  1002	        return EXIT_PRESTART_REFUSED
  1003	    os.mkdir(out_dir)
  1004	    os.mkdir(os.path.join(out_dir, "children"))
  1005	    STATE.update(out=out_dir, abs_end=abs_end, chunk_end=t_start + MAX_CHUNK,
  1006	                 remaining_reserve=CONTROL_RESERVE + len(ITEMS) * ITEM_RESERVE,
  1007	                 go={"path": go_path, "sha256": go_sha}, runner_sha=self_sha)
  1008	    launch = {
  1009	        "schema": "PA19-96H-PILOT-LAUNCH-v9", "host_clock": clock(), "owner": "ContinuePA19",
  1010	        "go": STATE["go"], "runner": {"path": os.path.abspath(__file__), "sha256": self_sha},
  1011	        "pid": os.getpid(), "ppid": os.getppid(), "pgid": os.getpgid(0), "cwd": os.getcwd(), "argv": sys.argv,
  1012	        "python": sys.version, "binaries": {"kissat": KISSAT, "drat_trim_lane_copy": DRAT, "drat_trim_old": DRAT_OLD},
  1013	        "inputs": {"encoder": ENC, "skeleton": SKEL, "items": ITEMS, "registry": REGISTRY,
  1014	                   "launch_contract": LAUNCH_CONTRACT, "binary_imports": BINARY_IMPORTS, "monitor": MONITOR,
  1015	                   "observer_source": OBSERVER_SOURCE_SHA},
  1016	        "absolute_end_epoch": abs_end, "max_chunk_end_epoch": t_start + MAX_CHUNK,
  1017	        "retained_output_reservation_bytes": STATE["remaining_reserve"],
  1018	        "reservation_breakdown": {"control_reserve": CONTROL_RESERVE, "item_reserve_each": ITEM_RESERVE},
  1019	        "output_dir": out_dir, "children_dir": os.path.join(out_dir, "children"),
  1020	        "limits": {"solver_s": SOLVER_SECONDS, "check_s": CHECK_SECONDS, "grace_s": GRACE, "term_wait_s": TERM_WAIT,
  1021	                   "stream_join_s": STREAM_JOIN, "proof_cap_hard": PROOF_CAP, "control_proof_cap_hard": CONTROL_PROOF_CAP,
  1022	                   "log_cap_hard": LOG_CAP, "lane_rss_trip": LANE_RSS_TRIP, "free_floor": FREE_FLOOR,
  1023	                   "fresh_base": FRESH_BASE},
  1024	        "exit_codes": {"0": "plan complete (each item terminal UNSAT/UNKNOWN); not a science verdict",
  1025	                       "10": "SAT candidate", "20": "controls failed", "30": "admission refused",
  1026	                       "40": "owner cancel", "50": "safety stop / latch / unsettled", "60": "exception",
  1027	                       "70": "prestart refused"},
  1028	    }
  1029	    run_record = os.path.join(out_dir, "launch-record.json")
  1030	    STATE["run_record"] = {"path": run_record, "sha256": write_x(run_record, launch)}
  1031	    print("LAUNCH_RECORD", run_record, flush=True)
  1032	    summary = {"schema": "PA19-96H-PILOT-SUMMARY-v9", "items": {}, "controls_ok": None, "stopped": None,
  1033	               "terminal_policy": "exit code reports runner execution only; per-item records carry the scientific "
  1034	                                  "status; an item counts only if qualify_unsat, the terminal gate and the commit "
  1035	                                  "boundary all pass; case closure is decided by the parent audit"}
  1036	    outcome = {"sat": False, "controls_failed": False, "refused": False, "exception": False, "complete": False}
  1037	    try:
  1038	        run_plan(summary, outcome)
  1039	    except BaseException as exc:
  1040	        outcome["exception"] = True
  1041	        summary["stopped"] = {"phase": "exception", "error": repr(exc)}
  1042	    finally:
  1043	        code = final_code(summary, outcome)
  1044	    return code

  1047	def run_plan(summary, outcome):
  1048	    """The plan body; every early stop is a plain return with the outcome flags set."""
  1049	    out_dir = STATE["out"]
  1050	    if sha_file(DRAT_OLD[0])[0] != DRAT_OLD[1] or sha_file(KISSAT[0])[0] != KISSAT[1]:
  1051	        raise RuntimeError("binary pin mismatch")
  1052	    if sha_file(DRAT)[0] != DRAT_OLD[1] or os.lstat(DRAT).st_ino == os.lstat(DRAT_OLD[0]).st_ino:
  1053	        raise RuntimeError("vendored drat-trim mismatch or shared inode")
  1054	    ok, recs = controls()
  1055	    summary["controls_ok"] = ok
  1056	    summary["controls_record_sha256"] = write_x(os.path.join(out_dir, "controls.json"), recs)
  1057	    STATE["remaining_reserve"] -= CONTROL_RESERVE
  1058	    if not ok:
  1059	        outcome["controls_failed"] = True
  1060	        summary["stopped"] = {"phase": "controls"}
  1061	        return
  1062	    read_pinned(ENC[0], ENC[1])
  1063	    sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
  1064	    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("encode_skel_b_lane", ENC[0])
  1065	    E = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
  1066	    spec.loader.exec_module(E)
  1067	    D = json.loads(read_pinned(*SKEL))
  1068	    special = [p for p in D["profiles"] if list(p) != P0]
  1069	    for it in ITEMS:
  1070	        label = it["id"].replace(":", "_")
  1071	        rec = {"id": it["id"], "status": "UNKNOWN", "count_for_scientific_coverage": False,
  1072	               "host_clock_start": clock()}
  1073	        rec_path = os.path.join(out_dir, label + ".record.json")
  1074	        Ej = json.loads(read_pinned(*it["list"]))
  1075	        if not (Ej["num_placements"] == len(Ej["placements"]) == it["denominator"]):
  1076	            raise RuntimeError("list denominator mismatch " + it["id"])
  1077	        sk = D["skeletons"][it["b"]]
  1078	        nv, cl = E.build_partbatch(D["c0"], [tuple(t) for t in sk["types"]], sk["empty"], special,
  1079	                                   [Ej["placements"][i] for i in range(it["lo"], it["hi"] + 1)])
  1080	        cnf = E.cnf_bytes(nv, cl)
  1081	        del cl
  1082	        got = (hashlib.sha256(cnf).hexdigest(), len(cnf), cnf.split(b"\n", 1)[0].decode())
  1083	        if got != it["cnf"]:
  1084	            raise RuntimeError("CNF digest mismatch %r" % (got,))
  1085	        cnf_path = os.path.join(out_dir, label + ".cnf")
  1086	        write_bytes_x(cnf_path, cnf)
  1087	        del cnf
  1088	        if sha_file(cnf_path) != (it["cnf"][0], it["cnf"][1]):
  1089	            raise RuntimeError("CNF file re-hash mismatch")
  1090	        inputs = {"item": it["id"], "cnf_pin": [it["cnf"][0], it["cnf"][1]], "list_pin": it["list"][1],
  1091	                  "skeleton_pin": SKEL[1], "encoder_pin": ENC[1]}
  1092	        proof_path = os.path.join(out_dir, label + ".drat")
  1093	        ks, kl, refused = run_kissat(cnf_path, proof_path, label, SOLVER_SECONDS, PROOF_CAP,
  1094	                                     PROOF_CAP + LOG_CAP + RECORD_ALLOW // 2, inputs)
  1095	        if ks is None:
  1096	            rec.update(reason="solver admission refused", refusal=refused, host_clock_end=clock())
  1097	            write_x(rec_path, rec)
  1098	            summary["items"][it["id"]] = {"status": "UNKNOWN", "reason": "solver admission refused"}
  1099	            summary["stopped"] = {"phase": "solver_admission", "item": it["id"], "why": refused}
  1100	            outcome["refused"] = True
  1101	            return
  1102	        rec["solver"] = ks
  1103	        rec["solver_status_lines"] = [s for s in kl if s.startswith("s ")]
  1104	        if ks["returncode"] == 10 and rec["solver_status_lines"] == ["s SATISFIABLE"] and ks["stop"] == "self" \
  1105	                and ks["settled"]:
  1106	            rec["status"] = "SAT_CANDIDATE"
  1107	            rec["sat"] = verify_sat(cnf_path, kl, label)
  1108	            rec["host_clock_end"] = clock()
  1109	            write_x(rec_path, rec)
  1110	            summary["items"][it["id"]] = {"status": "SAT_CANDIDATE", "sat": rec["sat"]}
  1111	            summary["stopped"] = {"phase": "sat_candidate", "item": it["id"]}
  1112	            outcome["sat"] = True
  1113	            return
  1114	        sink_ok = sink_complete(ks["proof_sink"])
  1115	        if sink_ok:
  1116	            rec["proof"] = {"sha256": ks["proof_sink"]["sha256_of_written"], "bytes": ks["proof_sink"]["written"]}
  1117	        if ks["returncode"] == 20 and ks["stop"] == "self" and ks["settled"] and sink_ok and not LATCH \
  1118	                and not CANCEL.is_set():
  1119	            dr, dl, refused = run_drat(cnf_path, proof_path, label, CHECK_SECONDS, LOG_CAP + RECORD_ALLOW // 2,
  1120	                                       dict(inputs, proof_pin=[rec["proof"]["sha256"], rec["proof"]["bytes"]]))
  1121	            if dr is None:
  1122	                rec.update(reason="checker admission refused", refusal=refused, host_clock_end=clock())
  1123	                write_x(rec_path, rec)
  1124	                summary["items"][it["id"]] = {"status": "UNKNOWN", "reason": "checker admission refused"}
  1125	                summary["stopped"] = {"phase": "checker_admission", "item": it["id"], "why": refused}
  1126	                outcome["refused"] = True
  1127	                return
  1128	            rec["checker"] = dr
  1129	            rec["checker_status_lines"] = [s for s in dl if s.startswith("s ")]
  1130	            rec["checker_parse_lines"] = [s for s in dl if s.startswith("c parsing") or s.startswith("c finished parsing")]
  1131	            post_cnf, post_proof = sha_file(cnf_path), sha_file(proof_path)
  1132	            rec["post_check_hashes"] = {"cnf": list(post_cnf), "proof": list(post_proof)}
  1133	            rec["defects"] = qualify_unsat(it["cnf"], ks, kl, rec["proof"]["sha256"], rec["proof"]["bytes"],
  1134	                                           PROOF_CAP, dr, dl, post_cnf, post_proof)
  1135	            rec["host_clock_end"] = clock()
  1136	            rec["terminal_gate"] = terminal_gate()
  1137	            unsat = None
  1138	            if not rec["defects"] and not rec["terminal_gate"] and not LATCH:
  1139	                unsat = dict(rec, status="UNSAT", count_for_scientific_coverage=True,
  1140	                             commit="UNSAT chosen at an explicit commit boundary with no earlier cancel stamp or latch")
  1141	            commit = commit_record(rec_path, dict(rec, commit="UNKNOWN record"), unsat)
  1142	            rec["status"] = "UNSAT" if commit["committed_unsat"] else "UNKNOWN"
  1143	            summary.setdefault("commits", {})[it["id"]] = commit
  1144	        else:
  1145	            rec["reason"] = "no settled self-exit rc20 verdict with a complete proof sink (or latch/cancel)"
  1146	            rec["host_clock_end"] = clock()
  1147	            write_x(rec_path, rec)
  1148	        summary["items"][it["id"]] = {k: rec.get(k) for k in ("status", "defects", "terminal_gate", "reason", "proof")}
  1149	        STATE["remaining_reserve"] -= ITEM_RESERVE
  1150	        if LATCH or CANCEL.is_set():
  1151	            summary["stopped"] = {"phase": "latch_or_cancel", "item": it["id"]}
  1152	            return
  1153	    outcome["complete"] = True
  1154	
  1155	
  1156	def final_code(summary, outcome):
  1157	    """Computed exit code (never inferred from a plain return) and the computed settlement summary."""
  1158	    out_dir = STATE["out"]
  1159	    if outcome["sat"]:
  1160	        code = EXIT_SAT_CANDIDATE
  1161	    elif LATCH:
  1162	        code = EXIT_SAFETY_STOP
  1163	    elif outcome["exception"]:
  1164	        code = EXIT_EXCEPTION
  1165	    elif CANCEL.is_set():
  1166	        code = EXIT_CANCELLED
  1167	    elif outcome["controls_failed"]:
  1168	        code = EXIT_CONTROLS_FAILED
  1169	    elif outcome["refused"]:
  1170	        code = EXIT_ADMISSION_REFUSED
  1171	    elif outcome["complete"]:
  1172	        code = EXIT_PLAN_COMPLETE
  1173	    else:
  1174	        code = EXIT_EXCEPTION
  1175	    children = [c.settled_rec if c.settled_rec is not None else {"seq": c.seq, "label": c.label, "pid": c.pid,
  1176	                                                                   "settled": False, "note": "never settled"}
  1177	                for c in OWNED]
  1178	    all_settled = all(r.get("settled") for r in children)
  1179	    if not all_settled and code != EXIT_SAT_CANDIDATE:
  1180	        code = EXIT_SAFETY_STOP
  1181	    summary.update(exit_code=code, latch=list(LATCH), cancel=CANCEL.is_set(), cancel_order=STATE["cancel_order"],
  1182	                   children=[{k: r.get(k) for k in ("seq", "label", "pid", "returncode", "stop", "settled")}
  1183	                             for r in children],
  1184	                   all_children_settled=all_settled)
  1185	    try:
  1186	        summary["host_clock_end"] = clock()
  1187	        write_x(os.path.join(out_dir, "summary.json"), summary)
  1188	    except BaseException as exc:
  1189	        print("SUMMARY WRITE FAILED %r" % exc, flush=True)
  1190	        code = EXIT_SAFETY_STOP
  1191	    print("SUMMARY", os.path.join(out_dir, "summary.json"), "exit", code, flush=True)
  1192	    return code
  1193

  1195	if __name__ == "__main__":
  1196	    sys.exit(main())

 succeeded in 0ms:
   301	        except Exception as exc:
   302	            return ["malformed monitor line %d: %r (%r)" % (i, ln[:160], exc)]
   303	    if not rows:
   304	        return ["no monitor samples"]
   305	    for a, b in zip(rows, rows[1:]):
   306	        if not b[0] > a[0]:
   307	            return ["monitor timestamps not strictly increasing"]
   308	    why = []
   309	    now = time.time()
   310	    t_last, free_last = rows[-1]
   311	    if not (0 <= now - t_last <= 30):
   312	        why.append(f"monitor last sample age {now - t_last:.1f}s outside [0,30]")
   313	    win = [r for r in rows if now - 300 <= r[0] <= now]
   314	    if len(win) < 25:
   315	        why.append(f"only {len(win)} samples in [now-300, now]")
   316	    elif win[-1][0] - win[0][0] < 290:
   317	        why.append(f"window span {win[-1][0] - win[0][0]:.1f}s < 290 s")
   318	    if free_last < 13_500_000_000 + projected:
   319	        why.append(f"last free {free_last} < 13.5e9 + projected {projected}")
   320	    allowed = max(300_000_000, (free_last - 13_500_000_000) // 12)
   321	    peak, drop = None, 0
   322	    for _, fr in win:
   323	        peak = fr if peak is None else max(peak, fr)
   324	        drop = max(drop, peak - fr)
   325	    if drop > allowed:
   326	        why.append(f"peak-to-later drop {drop} > allowed {allowed}")
   327	    return why

   330	def full_admission(projected, child_deadline):
   331	    """Blocking part of admission (registry, monitor, statvfs, fit). Returns refusal reasons."""
   332	    why = []
   333	    try:
   334	        why += registry_refusals()
   335	        why += stability_refusals(projected)
   336	        fb = free_bytes()
   337	        if fb < FRESH_BASE + projected:
   338	            why.append(f"fresh free {fb} < 12.2e9 + {projected}")
   339	        if fb - STATE["remaining_reserve"] < FREE_FLOOR:
   340	            why.append(f"free {fb} - remaining reservations {STATE['remaining_reserve']} < 12e9")
   341	    except Exception as exc:
   342	        why.append("admission exception %r" % exc)
   343	    if child_deadline > STATE["abs_end"] or child_deadline + SETTLE + MARGIN > STATE["chunk_end"] \
   344	            or child_deadline > OWNED_SETTLED_BY - 180:
   345	        why.append("per-child chunk fit / absolute end")
   346	    return why

   348	
   349	class ProofSink:
   350	    """Owned FIFO reader writing the proof FILE with a HARD byte cap."""
   351	
   352	    def __init__(self, fifo_path, file_path, cap):
   353	        if os.path.lexists(fifo_path) or os.path.lexists(file_path):
   354	            raise RuntimeError("proof fifo/file path exists")
   355	        os.mkfifo(fifo_path, 0o600)
   356	        self.fifo_path, self.file_path, self.cap = fifo_path, file_path, cap
   357	        self.file = open(file_path, "xb")
   358	        self.sha = hashlib.sha256()
   359	        self.written = self.received = self.discarded = 0
   360	        self.over_cap = self.connected = self.eof = False
   361	        self.error = None
   362	        self.unblocked_by_runner = False
   363	        self.finished = None
   364	        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self._run, daemon=True)
   365	        self.thread.start()
   366	
   367	    def _run(self):
   368	        try:
   369	            fd = os.open(self.fifo_path, os.O_RDONLY)  # blocks until a writer opens the FIFO
   370	            self.connected = True
   371	            try:
   372	                while True:
   373	                    b = os.read(fd, 1 << 20)
   374	                    if not b:
   375	                        self.eof = True
   376	                        break
   377	                    self.received += len(b)
   378	                    if not self.over_cap and self.written + len(b) <= self.cap:
   379	                        self.file.write(b)
   380	                        self.sha.update(b)
   381	                        self.written += len(b)
   382	                    else:
   383	                        self.over_cap = True       # never write beyond the cap; drain so the producer can exit
   384	                        self.discarded += len(b)
   385	            finally:
   386	                os.close(fd)
   387	        except Exception as exc:
   388	            self.error = repr(exc)
   389	        finally:
   390	            try:
   391	                self.file.flush()
   392	                os.fsync(self.file.fileno())
   393	                self.file.close()
   394	            except Exception as exc:
   395	                self.error = (self.error or "") + " close:" + repr(exc)
   396	
   397	    def finish(self):
   398	        """After the producer is reaped: unblock a never-connected reader, bounded join; latches on
   399	        any unsettled thread, sink I/O error or re-hash failure."""
   400	        if self.finished is not None:
   401	            return self.finished
   402	        deadline = time.time() + SINK_UNBLOCK
   403	        while self.thread.is_alive() and not self.connected and time.time() < deadline:
   404	            try:
   405	                fd = os.open(self.fifo_path, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_NONBLOCK)
   406	                self.unblocked_by_runner = True
   407	                os.close(fd)
   408	                break
   409	            except OSError as exc:
   410	                if exc.errno != errno.ENXIO:
   411	                    self.error = (self.error or "") + " unblock:" + repr(exc)
   412	                    break
   413	                time.sleep(0.05)
   414	        self.thread.join(STREAM_JOIN)
   415	        alive = self.thread.is_alive()
   416	        rec = {"fifo": self.fifo_path, "file": self.file_path, "cap": self.cap, "connected": self.connected,
   417	               "eof": self.eof, "received": self.received, "written": self.written, "discarded": self.discarded,
   418	               "over_cap": self.over_cap, "error": self.error, "unblocked_by_runner": self.unblocked_by_runner,
   419	               "thread_alive_after_join": alive, "sha256_of_written": self.sha.hexdigest()}
   420	        if alive:
   421	            latch("proof sink thread unsettled: " + self.fifo_path)
   422	        else:
   423	            try:
   424	                rec["file_rehash"] = list(sha_file(self.file_path))
   425	            except Exception as exc:
   426	                rec["file_rehash_error"] = repr(exc)
   427	                latch("proof file re-hash failed %r" % exc)
   428	        if self.error:
   429	            latch("proof sink I/O error %s" % self.error)
   430	        rec["sink_settled"] = (not alive and self.error is None and "file_rehash" in rec)
   431	        self.finished = rec
   432	        return rec
   433	
   434	
   435	def emergency_cleanup(proc, why):
   436	    """r2#2: minimal-allocation owned cleanup of a just-spawned child; never escapes before wait()."""
   437	    try:
   438	        latch("post-Popen guard: %r" % (why,))
   439	    except BaseException:
   440	        pass
   441	    try:
   442	        proc.send_signal(signal.SIGTERM)  # single SIGTERM to the unreaped owned child
   443	    except BaseException:
   444	        pass
   445	    end = time.time() + TERM_WAIT
   446	    while proc.poll() is None and time.time() < end:
   447	        time.sleep(0.1)
   448	    proc.wait()  # quarantine semantics: no further signal, keep waiting
   449	
   450	
   451	class Child:
   452	    """One owned native child in its own session; HARD byte-capped capture; exclusive records."""
   453	
   454	    def __init__(self, argv, label, wall, reserve, inputs, sink):
   455	        STATE["seq"] += 1
   456	        self.seq = STATE["seq"]
   457	        self.argv, self.label, self.sink_obj = argv, label, sink
   458	        base = os.path.join(STATE["out"], "children", "%02d-%s" % (self.seq, label))
   459	        self.out_path, self.launch_path, self.settle_path = base + ".out", base + ".launch.json", base + ".settle.json"
   460	        self.quarantine_path = base + ".QUARANTINE.json"
   461	        self.captured, self.over_cap, self.reader_error = 0, False, None
   462	        self.term_sent_at, self.stop, self.reader, self.settled_rec = None, None, None, None
   463	        self.identity_at_start, self.descendants = None, []
   464	        self.inputs, self.reserve = inputs, reserve
   465	        self.capture = open(self.out_path, "xb")  # outputs prepared BEFORE admission and spawn
   466	        self.deadline = time.time() + wall + GRACE
   467	        self.deadline = min(self.deadline, STATE["abs_end"])
   468	        why = full_admission(reserve, self.deadline)
   469	        # r2#5: cancel/latch and the frozen deadline are rechecked immediately before Popen.
   470	        if LATCH:
   471	            why.append("latched: %r" % LATCH)
   472	        if CANCEL.is_set():
   473	            why.append("owner cancel")
   474	        if time.time() + wall > self.deadline:
   475	            why.append("frozen child deadline already too close")
   476	        if why:
   477	            self.capture.close()
   478	            self.refused = why
   479	            self.proc = None
   480	            return
   481	        self.refused = None
   482	        proc = subprocess.Popen(argv, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
   483	                                start_new_session=True, close_fds=True, cwd=LANE + "/tmp", env=CHILD_ENV)
   484	        try:
   485	            self.proc = proc
   486	            self.pid = self.pgid = proc.pid
   487	            self.started = time.time()
   488	            OWNED.append(self)
   489	            self.identity_at_start = identity_checked(self.pid, os.getpid())
   490	            write_x(self.launch_path, {"seq": self.seq, "label": label, "runner_pid": os.getpid(), "pid": self.pid,
   491	                                       "pgid": self.pgid, "identity": self.identity_at_start, "argv": argv,
   492	                                       "cwd": LANE + "/tmp", "start_epoch": self.started,
   493	                                       "absolute_deadline_epoch": self.deadline, "capture": self.out_path,
   494	                                       "capture_cap": LOG_CAP, "retained_reservation_bytes": reserve,
   495	                                       "inputs": inputs, "run_launch_record": STATE["run_record"],
   496	                                       "go": STATE["go"], "runner_sha256": STATE["runner_sha"],
   497	                                       "binaries": {"kissat": KISSAT, "drat_trim": [DRAT, DRAT_OLD[1]]}})
   498	            print("CHILD_LAUNCH", self.launch_path, flush=True)
   499	            self.reader = threading.Thread(target=self._read, daemon=True)
   500	            self.reader.start()
   501	        except BaseException as exc:
   502	            self.term_sent_at = time.time()
   503	            self.stop = "registration_failure"
   504	            emergency_cleanup(proc, exc)
   505	            try:
   506	                self.settle()
   507	            except BaseException:
   508	                pass
   509	            raise
   510	
   511	    def _read(self):
   512	        try:
   513	            while True:
   514	                b = self.proc.stdout.read1(65536)
   515	                if not b:
   516	                    break
   517	                room = LOG_CAP - self.captured
   518	                if room > 0:
   519	                    self.capture.write(b[:room])
   520	                self.captured += len(b)
   521	                if self.captured > LOG_CAP:
   522	                    self.over_cap = True
   523	        except Exception as exc:
   524	            self.reader_error = repr(exc)
   525	        finally:
   526	            try:
   527	                self.capture.flush()
   528	                os.fsync(self.capture.fileno())
   529	                self.capture.close()
   530	                self.proc.stdout.close()
   531	            except Exception as exc:
   532	                self.reader_error = (self.reader_error or "") + " close:" + repr(exc)
   533	
   534	    def alive(self):
   535	        return self.proc.poll() is None
   536	
   537	    def term(self, reason):
   538	        if self.term_sent_at is None and self.proc.returncode is None:
   539	            self.stop = reason
   540	            self.term_sent_at = time.time()
   541	            self.proc.send_signal(signal.SIGTERM)  # Popen polls first; never signals a reaped PID
   542	
   543	    def census(self):
   544	        """r2#7: other processes with ppid==child or pgid==child (excluding the child itself)."""
   545	        rows = ps_table()
   546	        found = [r for r in rows if r[0] != self.pid and (r[1] == self.pid or r[2] == self.pgid)]
   547	        rss = sum(r[3] for r in rows if r[0] == self.pid or r in found) * 1024
   548	        return found, rss
   549	
   550	    def settle(self):
   551	        """r2#2/#3: always reaches wait(); settlement final only after streams, sink, hashes and receipt."""
   552	        if self.settled_rec is not None:
   553	            return self.settled_rec
   554	        quarantine, problems = None, []
   555	        if self.term_sent_at is not None:
   556	            end = self.term_sent_at + TERM_WAIT
   557	            while self.proc.poll() is None and time.time() < end:
   558	                time.sleep(0.1)
   559	            if self.proc.poll() is None:
   560	                latch("quarantine pid %d" % self.pid)
   561	                quarantine = self.quarantine_path
   562	                try:
   563	                    write_x(quarantine, {"pid": self.pid, "pgid": self.pgid, "argv": self.argv,
   564	                                         "epoch": time.time(), "note": "survived SIGTERM + 60 s; no further "
   565	                                         "signal; runner keeps waiting and starts no new child"})
   566	                except BaseException as exc:
   567	                    problems.append("quarantine receipt failed %r" % exc)
   568	        rc = self.proc.wait()
   569	        stream_alive = None
   570	        if self.reader is not None:
   571	            self.reader.join(STREAM_JOIN)
   572	            stream_alive = self.reader.is_alive()
   573	        else:
   574	            problems.append("no capture reader")
   575	            try:
   576	                self.capture.close()
   577	            except Exception as exc:
   578	                problems.append("capture close %r" % exc)
   579	        group_empty = None
   580	        try:
   581	            os.killpg(self.pgid, 0)
   582	            group_empty = False
   583	        except ProcessLookupError:
   584	            group_empty = True
   585	        except BaseException as exc:
   586	            problems.append("killpg probe %r" % exc)
   587	        try:
   588	            after, _ = self.census()
   589	            if after:
   590	                problems.append("descendants after reap %r" % after)
   591	        except BaseException as exc:
   592	            problems.append("post-reap census failed %r" % exc)
   593	        if self.descendants:
   594	            problems.append("descendants seen during run %r" % self.descendants[:5])
   595	        sink_rec = None
   596	        if self.sink_obj is not None:
   597	            try:
   598	                sink_rec = self.sink_obj.finish()
   599	                if not sink_rec["sink_settled"]:
   600	                    problems.append("proof sink not settled")

   349	class ProofSink:
   350	    """Owned FIFO reader writing the proof FILE with a HARD byte cap."""
   351	
   352	    def __init__(self, fifo_path, file_path, cap):
   353	        if os.path.lexists(fifo_path) or os.path.lexists(file_path):
   354	            raise RuntimeError("proof fifo/file path exists")
   355	        os.mkfifo(fifo_path, 0o600)
   356	        self.fifo_path, self.file_path, self.cap = fifo_path, file_path, cap
   357	        self.file = open(file_path, "xb")
   358	        self.sha = hashlib.sha256()
   359	        self.written = self.received = self.discarded = 0
   360	        self.over_cap = self.connected = self.eof = False
   361	        self.error = None
   362	        self.unblocked_by_runner = False
   363	        self.finished = None
   364	        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self._run, daemon=True)
   365	        self.thread.start()
   366	
   367	    def _run(self):
   368	        try:
   369	            fd = os.open(self.fifo_path, os.O_RDONLY)  # blocks until a writer opens the FIFO
   370	            self.connected = True
   371	            try:
   372	                while True:
   373	                    b = os.read(fd, 1 << 20)
   374	                    if not b:
   375	                        self.eof = True
   376	                        break
   377	                    self.received += len(b)
   378	                    if not self.over_cap and self.written + len(b) <= self.cap:
   379	                        self.file.write(b)
   380	                        self.sha.update(b)
   381	                        self.written += len(b)
   382	                    else:
   383	                        self.over_cap = True       # never write beyond the cap; drain so the producer can exit
   384	                        self.discarded += len(b)
   385	            finally:
   386	                os.close(fd)
   387	        except Exception as exc:
   388	            self.error = repr(exc)
   389	        finally:
   390	            try:
   391	                self.file.flush()
   392	                os.fsync(self.file.fileno())
   393	                self.file.close()
   394	            except Exception as exc:
   395	                self.error = (self.error or "") + " close:" + repr(exc)
   396	
   397	    def finish(self):
   398	        """After the producer is reaped: unblock a never-connected reader, bounded join; latches on
   399	        any unsettled thread, sink I/O error or re-hash failure."""
   400	        if self.finished is not None:
   401	            return self.finished
   402	        deadline = time.time() + SINK_UNBLOCK
   403	        while self.thread.is_alive() and not self.connected and time.time() < deadline:
   404	            try:
   405	                fd = os.open(self.fifo_path, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_NONBLOCK)
   406	                self.unblocked_by_runner = True
   407	                os.close(fd)
   408	                break
   409	            except OSError as exc:
   410	                if exc.errno != errno.ENXIO:
   411	                    self.error = (self.error or "") + " unblock:" + repr(exc)
   412	                    break
   413	                time.sleep(0.05)
   414	        self.thread.join(STREAM_JOIN)
   415	        alive = self.thread.is_alive()
   416	        rec = {"fifo": self.fifo_path, "file": self.file_path, "cap": self.cap, "connected": self.connected,
   417	               "eof": self.eof, "received": self.received, "written": self.written, "discarded": self.discarded,
   418	               "over_cap": self.over_cap, "error": self.error, "unblocked_by_runner": self.unblocked_by_runner,
   419	               "thread_alive_after_join": alive, "sha256_of_written": self.sha.hexdigest()}
   420	        if alive:
   421	            latch("proof sink thread unsettled: " + self.fifo_path)
   422	        else:
   423	            try:
   424	                rec["file_rehash"] = list(sha_file(self.file_path))
   425	            except Exception as exc:
   426	                rec["file_rehash_error"] = repr(exc)
   427	                latch("proof file re-hash failed %r" % exc)
   428	        if self.error:
   429	            latch("proof sink I/O error %s" % self.error)
   430	        rec["sink_settled"] = (not alive and self.error is None and "file_rehash" in rec)
   431	        self.finished = rec
   432	        return rec
   433	
   434	
   435	def emergency_cleanup(proc, why):
   436	    """r2#2: minimal-allocation owned cleanup of a just-spawned child; never escapes before wait()."""
   437	    try:
   438	        latch("post-Popen guard: %r" % (why,))
   439	    except BaseException:
   440	        pass
   441	    try:
   442	        proc.send_signal(signal.SIGTERM)  # single SIGTERM to the unreaped owned child
   443	    except BaseException:
   444	        pass
   445	    end = time.time() + TERM_WAIT
   446	    while proc.poll() is None and time.time() < end:
   447	        time.sleep(0.1)
   448	    proc.wait()  # quarantine semantics: no further signal, keep waiting
   449	
   450	
   451	class Child:
   452	    """One owned native child in its own session; HARD byte-capped capture; exclusive records."""
   453	
   454	    def __init__(self, argv, label, wall, reserve, inputs, sink):
   455	        STATE["seq"] += 1
   456	        self.seq = STATE["seq"]
   457	        self.argv, self.label, self.sink_obj = argv, label, sink
   458	        base = os.path.join(STATE["out"], "children", "%02d-%s" % (self.seq, label))
   459	        self.out_path, self.launch_path, self.settle_path = base + ".out", base + ".launch.json", base + ".settle.json"
   460	        self.quarantine_path = base + ".QUARANTINE.json"
   461	        self.captured, self.over_cap, self.reader_error = 0, False, None
   462	        self.term_sent_at, self.stop, self.reader, self.settled_rec = None, None, None, None
   463	        self.identity_at_start, self.descendants = None, []
   464	        self.inputs, self.reserve = inputs, reserve
   465	        self.capture = open(self.out_path, "xb")  # outputs prepared BEFORE admission and spawn
   466	        self.deadline = time.time() + wall + GRACE
   467	        self.deadline = min(self.deadline, STATE["abs_end"])
   468	        why = full_admission(reserve, self.deadline)
   469	        # r2#5: cancel/latch and the frozen deadline are rechecked immediately before Popen.
   470	        if LATCH:
   471	            why.append("latched: %r" % LATCH)
   472	        if CANCEL.is_set():
   473	            why.append("owner cancel")
   474	        if time.time() + wall > self.deadline:
   475	            why.append("frozen child deadline already too close")
   476	        if why:
   477	            self.capture.close()
   478	            self.refused = why
   479	            self.proc = None
   480	            return
   481	        self.refused = None
   482	        proc = subprocess.Popen(argv, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
   483	                                start_new_session=True, close_fds=True, cwd=LANE + "/tmp", env=CHILD_ENV)
   484	        try:
   485	            self.proc = proc
   486	            self.pid = self.pgid = proc.pid
   487	            self.started = time.time()
   488	            OWNED.append(self)
   489	            self.identity_at_start = identity_checked(self.pid, os.getpid())
   490	            write_x(self.launch_path, {"seq": self.seq, "label": label, "runner_pid": os.getpid(), "pid": self.pid,
   491	                                       "pgid": self.pgid, "identity": self.identity_at_start, "argv": argv,
   492	                                       "cwd": LANE + "/tmp", "start_epoch": self.started,
   493	                                       "absolute_deadline_epoch": self.deadline, "capture": self.out_path,
   494	                                       "capture_cap": LOG_CAP, "retained_reservation_bytes": reserve,
   495	                                       "inputs": inputs, "run_launch_record": STATE["run_record"],
   496	                                       "go": STATE["go"], "runner_sha256": STATE["runner_sha"],
   497	                                       "binaries": {"kissat": KISSAT, "drat_trim": [DRAT, DRAT_OLD[1]]}})
   498	            print("CHILD_LAUNCH", self.launch_path, flush=True)
   499	            self.reader = threading.Thread(target=self._read, daemon=True)
   500	            self.reader.start()
   501	        except BaseException as exc:
   502	            self.term_sent_at = time.time()
   503	            self.stop = "registration_failure"
   504	            emergency_cleanup(proc, exc)
   505	            try:
   506	                self.settle()
   507	            except BaseException:
   508	                pass
   509	            raise
   510	
   511	    def _read(self):
   512	        try:
   513	            while True:
   514	                b = self.proc.stdout.read1(65536)
   515	                if not b:
   516	                    break
   517	                room = LOG_CAP - self.captured
   518	                if room > 0:
   519	                    self.capture.write(b[:room])
   520	                self.captured += len(b)
   521	                if self.captured > LOG_CAP:
   522	                    self.over_cap = True
   523	        except Exception as exc:
   524	            self.reader_error = repr(exc)
   525	        finally:
   526	            try:
   527	                self.capture.flush()
   528	                os.fsync(self.capture.fileno())
   529	                self.capture.close()
   530	                self.proc.stdout.close()
   531	            except Exception as exc:
   532	                self.reader_error = (self.reader_error or "") + " close:" + repr(exc)
   533	
   534	    def alive(self):
   535	        return self.proc.poll() is None
   536	
   537	    def term(self, reason):
   538	        if self.term_sent_at is None and self.proc.returncode is None:
   539	            self.stop = reason
   540	            self.term_sent_at = time.time()
   541	            self.proc.send_signal(signal.SIGTERM)  # Popen polls first; never signals a reaped PID
   542	
   543	    def census(self):
   544	        """r2#7: other processes with ppid==child or pgid==child (excluding the child itself)."""
   545	        rows = ps_table()
   546	        found = [r for r in rows if r[0] != self.pid and (r[1] == self.pid or r[2] == self.pgid)]
   547	        rss = sum(r[3] for r in rows if r[0] == self.pid or r in found) * 1024
   548	        return found, rss
   549	
   550	    def settle(self):
   551	        """r2#2/#3: always reaches wait(); settlement final only after streams, sink, hashes and receipt."""
   552	        if self.settled_rec is not None:
   553	            return self.settled_rec
   554	        quarantine, problems = None, []
   555	        if self.term_sent_at is not None:
   556	            end = self.term_sent_at + TERM_WAIT
   557	            while self.proc.poll() is None and time.time() < end:
   558	                time.sleep(0.1)
   559	            if self.proc.poll() is None:
   560	                latch("quarantine pid %d" % self.pid)
   561	                quarantine = self.quarantine_path
   562	                try:
   563	                    write_x(quarantine, {"pid": self.pid, "pgid": self.pgid, "argv": self.argv,
   564	                                         "epoch": time.time(), "note": "survived SIGTERM + 60 s; no further "
   565	                                         "signal; runner keeps waiting and starts no new child"})
   566	                except BaseException as exc:
   567	                    problems.append("quarantine receipt failed %r" % exc)
   568	        rc = self.proc.wait()
   569	        stream_alive = None
   570	        if self.reader is not None:
   571	            self.reader.join(STREAM_JOIN)
   572	            stream_alive = self.reader.is_alive()
   573	        else:
   574	            problems.append("no capture reader")
   575	            try:
   576	                self.capture.close()
   577	            except Exception as exc:
   578	                problems.append("capture close %r" % exc)
   579	        group_empty = None
   580	        try:
   581	            os.killpg(self.pgid, 0)
   582	            group_empty = False
   583	        except ProcessLookupError:
   584	            group_empty = True
   585	        except BaseException as exc:
   586	            problems.append("killpg probe %r" % exc)
   587	        try:
   588	            after, _ = self.census()
   589	            if after:
   590	                problems.append("descendants after reap %r" % after)
   591	        except BaseException as exc:
   592	            problems.append("post-reap census failed %r" % exc)
   593	        if self.descendants:
   594	            problems.append("descendants seen during run %r" % self.descendants[:5])
   595	        sink_rec = None
   596	        if self.sink_obj is not None:
   597	            try:
   598	                sink_rec = self.sink_obj.finish()
   599	                if not sink_rec["sink_settled"]:
   600	                    problems.append("proof sink not settled")

   606	                out_sha = sha_file(self.out_path)[0]

   609	        rec = {"seq": self.seq, "label": self.label, "pid": self.pid, "pgid": self.pgid,
   610	               "identity_at_start": self.identity_at_start, "argv": self.argv, "returncode": rc,
   611	               "stop": self.stop or "self", "seconds": round(time.time() - self.started, 3),
   612	               "captured_bytes": self.captured, "capture_over_cap": self.over_cap, "reader_error": self.reader_error,
   613	               "stream_thread_alive_after_join": stream_alive, "group_empty_after_reap": group_empty,

   622	        except BaseException as exc:
   623	            rec["settle_receipt_error"] = repr(exc)
   624	        if not rec["settled"]:
   625	            latch("child %d %s not settled" % (self.seq, self.label))
   626	        self.settled_rec = rec
   627	        return rec

   630	def segments(path):
   631	    with open(path, "rb") as f:
   632	        txt = f.read().decode("utf-8", "replace")
   633	    return [s for s in re.split(r"[\r\n]", txt) if s != ""]
   634	
   635	
   636	def supervise(child, sink):
   637	    """Monitor until exit or a single trip; any monitor exception is itself a trip."""
   638	    me = os.getpid()
   639	    last_rss = last_reg = 0.0
   640	    peak = 0
   641	    while child.alive():
   642	        try:
   643	            now = time.time()
   644	            if CANCEL.is_set():
   645	                child.term("owner_cancel_sigterm")
   646	            elif now > child.deadline:
   647	                child.term("absolute_or_wall_deadline")
   648	            elif sink is not None and sink.over_cap:
   649	                child.term("proof_cap")
   650	            elif sink is not None and sink.error:
   651	                child.term("proof_sink_error")
   652	            elif child.over_cap:
   653	                child.term("capture_cap")
   654	            elif free_bytes() < FREE_FLOOR:
   655	                child.term("inflight_disk_floor")
   656	            if child.term_sent_at is None and now - last_rss >= 1.0:
   657	                last_rss = now
   658	                found, rss = child.census()
   659	                rows = ps_table()
   660	                runner_rss = sum(r[3] for r in rows if r[0] == me) * 1024
   661	                if found:
   662	                    child.descendants.extend(found)
   663	                    child.term("descendant_process")
   664	                total = rss + runner_rss
   665	                peak = max(peak, total)
   666	                if runner_rss == 0 or (rss == 0 and child.alive()):
   667	                    child.term("rss_unreadable")
   668	                elif total > LANE_RSS_TRIP:
   669	                    child.term("lane_rss")
   670	            if child.term_sent_at is None and now - last_reg >= 5.0:
   671	                last_reg = now
   672	                if registry_refusals():
   673	                    child.term("stop_registry")
   674	        except Exception as exc:
   675	            child.term("monitor_exception:%r" % exc)
   676	        if child.term_sent_at is not None:
   677	            break
   678	        time.sleep(0.25)
   679	    if child.stop is not None and child.stop not in NONLATCHING_TRIPS:
   680	        latch("trip %s on %s" % (child.stop, child.label))
   681	    return child.stop, peak
   682	
   683	
   684	def run_child(argv, label, wall, reserve, inputs, sink=None):
   685	    """Prepared outputs -> admission -> spawn -> supervise -> settle.  Returns (rec|None, lines, refusal)."""
   686	    ch = None
   687	    try:
   688	        ch = Child(argv, label, wall, reserve, inputs, sink)
   689	        if ch.refused:
   690	            return None, [], ch.refused
   691	        peak = None
   692	        try:
   693	            if CANCEL.is_set():
   694	                ch.term("owner_cancel_sigterm")
   695	            _, peak = supervise(ch, sink)
   696	        except BaseException as exc:
   697	            latch("runner exception during supervision %r" % exc)
   698	            ch.term("runner_exception")
   699	        rec = ch.settle()
   700	        rec["trip"] = ch.stop
   701	        rec["peak_sampled_lane_rss_bytes"] = peak
   702	        lines = segments(ch.out_path) if rec.get("stream_thread_alive_after_join") is False else []
   703	        return rec, lines, []
   704	    finally:
   705	        if ch is not None and getattr(ch, "proc", None) is not None and ch.settled_rec is None:
   706	            ch.settle()  # any escape above still reaps and settles the owned child
   707	        if sink is not None and (ch is None or getattr(ch, "proc", None) is None):
   708	            sink.finish()  # never-spawned producer: unblock and join the reader
   709

   711	def run_kissat(cnf_path, proof_file, label, seconds, cap, reserve, inputs):
   712	    argv = [KISSAT[0], "-q", "-f", "--seed=1", "--time=%d" % seconds, cnf_path]
   713	    sink = None
   714	    if proof_file is not None:
   715	        fifo_dir = os.path.join(STATE["out"], "fifo")
   716	        os.makedirs(fifo_dir, exist_ok=True)
   717	        sink = ProofSink(os.path.join(fifo_dir, label + ".proof.fifo"), proof_file, cap)
   718	        argv.append(sink.fifo_path)
   719	    return run_child(argv, label + ".kissat", seconds, reserve, dict(inputs, proof_file=proof_file, proof_cap=cap), sink)

   722	def run_drat(cnf_path, proof_path, label, seconds, reserve, inputs):
   723	    return run_child([DRAT, cnf_path, proof_path, "-i", "-t", str(seconds)], label + ".drat", seconds, reserve,
   724	                     dict(inputs, cnf=cnf_path, proof=proof_path))

   727	def sink_complete(sink_rec):
   728	    return (sink_rec is not None and sink_rec.get("sink_settled") and sink_rec["connected"] and sink_rec["eof"]
   729	            and not sink_rec["over_cap"] and sink_rec["error"] is None and not sink_rec["unblocked_by_runner"]
   730	            and sink_rec["discarded"] == 0 and sink_rec["received"] == sink_rec["written"]
   731	            and sink_rec.get("file_rehash") == [sink_rec["sha256_of_written"], sink_rec["written"]])

   734	def qualify_unsat(cnf_expect, solver_rec, kissat_lines, proof_sha, proof_bytes, proof_cap, drat_rec, drat_lines,
   735	                  cnf_post, proof_post):
   736	    bad = []
   737	    if solver_rec["returncode"] != 20 or solver_rec["stop"] != "self":
   738	        bad.append("solver not self-exit rc20")
   739	    if [s for s in kissat_lines if s.startswith("s ")] != ["s UNSATISFIABLE"]:
   740	        bad.append("solver status lines not exactly ['s UNSATISFIABLE']")
   741	    if not sink_complete(solver_rec.get("proof_sink")):
   742	        bad.append("proof sink incomplete/over cap/error")
   743	    if proof_bytes <= 0 or proof_bytes > proof_cap:
   744	        bad.append("proof size outside (0, cap]")
   745	    if drat_rec["returncode"] != 0 or drat_rec["stop"] != "self":
   746	        bad.append("checker not self-exit rc0")
   747	    st = [s for s in drat_lines if s.startswith("s ")]
   748	    if st != ["s VERIFIED"]:
   749	        bad.append("checker status lines %r" % st)
   750	    v, c = cnf_expect[2].split()[2:4]
   751	    if ("c parsing input formula with %s variables and %s clauses" % (v, c)) not in drat_lines:
   752	        bad.append("checker parse header line missing/mismatched")
   753	    if ("c finished parsing, read %d bytes from proof file" % proof_bytes) not in drat_lines:
   754	        bad.append("checker-consumed proof bytes != proof file bytes")
   755	    for pat in ("early EOF", "clauses less than expected", "trivial UNSAT", "ERROR", "MEMOUT",
   756	                "wrong binary prefix", "no empty clause detected", "NOT VERIFIED", "TIMEOUT"):
   757	        if any(pat in s for s in drat_lines):
   758	            bad.append("checker output contains %r" % pat)
   759	    if cnf_post != (cnf_expect[0], cnf_expect[1]):
   760	        bad.append("CNF file digest/bytes != expected")
   761	    if proof_post != (proof_sha, proof_bytes):
   762	        bad.append("proof file digest/bytes != sink measurement")
   763	    for rec in (solver_rec, drat_rec):
   764	        if not rec["settled"] or rec["capture_over_cap"]:
   765	            bad.append("settlement/capture defect seq %s" % rec["seq"])
   766	    return bad
   767	
   768	
   769	def verify_sat(cnf_path, kissat_lines, label):
   770	    """Two independent checks of a SAT model: clause satisfaction and both PA19 definitions."""
   771	    lits = set()
   772	    for s in kissat_lines:
   773	        if s.startswith("v "):
   774	            lits.update(int(x) for x in s[2:].split() if x != "0")
   775	    sat_all = True
   776	    with open(cnf_path, "rb") as f:
   777	        for ln in f:
   778	            if ln.startswith(b"p") or ln.startswith(b"c"):
   779	                continue
   780	            cl = [int(x) for x in ln.split()[:-1]]
   781	            if not any(l in lits for l in cl):
   782	                sat_all = False
   783	                break
   784	    A = [[None] * 8 for _ in range(19)]
   785	    cells_ok = True
   786	    for r in range(19):
   787	        for c in range(8):
   788	            ss = [s for s in range(6) if (1 + 48 * r + 6 * c + s) in lits]
   789	            if len(ss) != 1:
   790	                cells_ok = False
   791	            else:
   792	                A[r][c] = ss[0]
   793	    rowpair_ok = cells_ok and all(sum(A[r][c] == A[q][c] for c in range(8)) <= 1
   794	                                  for r in range(19) for q in range(r + 1, 19))
   795	    colpair_ok = cells_ok and all(len({(A[r][c], A[r][d]) for r in range(19)}) == 19
   796	                                  for c in range(8) for d in range(c + 1, 8))
   797	    cand = os.path.join(STATE["out"], label + ".SAT-CANDIDATE.json")
   798	    write_x(cand, {"host_clock": clock(), "array": A, "clauses_all_satisfied": sat_all,
   799	                   "cells_exactly_one": cells_ok, "row_pair_agreements_le_1": rowpair_ok,
   800	                   "column_pair_ordered_pairs_distinct": colpair_ok,
   801	                   "status": "CANDIDATE_UNVERIFIED - notify parent immediately"})
   802	    latch_path = os.path.join(LANE, "control", "SAT-CANDIDATE-" + label)
   803	    write_bytes_x(latch_path, cand.encode())
   804	    return {"candidate": cand, "latch": latch_path, "clauses_all_satisfied": sat_all, "cells": cells_ok,
   805	            "rowpair": rowpair_ok, "colpair": colpair_ok}

   807	
   808	def php_cnf(p, h):
   809	    """Pigeonhole: p pigeons into h holes; UNSAT iff p > h."""
   810	    var = lambda i, j: i * h + j + 1  # noqa: E731
   811	    cl = [[var(i, j) for j in range(h)] for i in range(p)]
   812	    for j in range(h):
   813	        for i in range(p):
   814	            for k in range(i + 1, p):
   815	                cl.append([-var(i, j), -var(k, j)])
   816	    return ("p cnf %d %d\n" % (p * h, len(cl)) + "".join(" ".join(map(str, c)) + " 0\n" for c in cl)).encode()
   817	
   818	
   819	def healthy(rec):
   820	    """A control child must have exited by itself (rc>=0), with no trip, fully settled."""
   821	    return rec is not None and rec["stop"] == "self" and rec["settled"] and not rec["capture_over_cap"] \
   822	        and rec["returncode"] is not None and rec["returncode"] >= 0
   823	
   824	
   825	def controls():
   826	    """Same-binary positive and intended-reason mutation controls; stops at the first refused,
   827	    unhealthy or not-as-intended control (later controls are not started)."""
   828	    recs = {}
   829	    d = os.path.join(STATE["out"], "controls")
   830	    os.mkdir(d)
   831	    per_child = LOG_CAP + CONTROL_PROOF_CAP + RECORD_ALLOW // 8
   832	
   833	    def fail(name, why):
   834	        recs[name] = dict(recs.get(name, {}), failed=why)
   835	        return False, recs
   836	
   837	    def pin(path):
   838	        return list(sha_file(path))

   941	
   942	def terminal_gate():
   943	    """Registry and disk evidence collected BEFORE the commit boundary; any failure latches (r2#4)."""
   944	    why = []
   945	    try:
   946	        why += registry_refusals()
   947	        if free_bytes() < FREE_FLOOR:
   948	            why.append("free below in-flight floor at terminal gate")
   949	    except Exception as exc:
   950	        why.append("terminal gate exception %r" % exc)
   951	    for w in why:
   952	        latch("terminal gate: " + w)
   953	    return why
   954	
   955	
   956	def commit_record(path, rec_unknown, rec_unsat):
   957	    """r2#1: explicit commit boundary.  Both serializations and the O_EXCL fd exist before the boundary;
   958	    the boundary is one atomic next(ORDER); a cancel stamped before it forces UNKNOWN."""
   959	    unknown_bytes = json.dumps(rec_unknown, indent=1, sort_keys=True).encode()
   960	    unsat_bytes = None if rec_unsat is None else json.dumps(rec_unsat, indent=1, sort_keys=True).encode()
   961	    fd = os.open(path, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL, 0o644)
   962	    try:
   963	        boundary = next(ORDER)
   964	        co = STATE["cancel_order"]
   965	        chosen = unsat_bytes if (unsat_bytes is not None and not LATCH and (co is None or co > boundary)) else unknown_bytes
   966	        view = memoryview(chosen)
   967	        while view:
   968	            k = os.write(fd, view)
   969	            view = view[k:]
   970	        os.fsync(fd)
   971	    finally:
   972	        os.close(fd)
   973	    return {"boundary": boundary, "cancel_order": STATE["cancel_order"], "committed_unsat": chosen is unsat_bytes,
   974	            "sha256": hashlib.sha256(chosen).hexdigest()}
