  1124	                summary["items"][it["id"]] = {"status": "UNKNOWN", "reason": "checker admission refused"}
  1125	                summary["stopped"] = {"phase": "checker_admission", "item": it["id"], "why": refused}
  1126	                outcome["refused"] = True
  1127	                return
  1128	            rec["checker"] = dr
  1129	            rec["checker_status_lines"] = [s for s in dl if s.startswith("s ")]
  1130	            rec["checker_parse_lines"] = [s for s in dl if s.startswith("c parsing") or s.startswith("c finished parsing")]
  1131	            post_cnf, post_proof = sha_file(cnf_path), sha_file(proof_path)
  1132	            rec["post_check_hashes"] = {"cnf": list(post_cnf), "proof": list(post_proof)}
  1133	            rec["defects"] = qualify_unsat(it["cnf"], ks, kl, rec["proof"]["sha256"], rec["proof"]["bytes"],
  1134	                                           PROOF_CAP, dr, dl, post_cnf, post_proof)
  1135	            rec["host_clock_end"] = clock()
  1136	            rec["terminal_gate"] = terminal_gate()
  1137	            unsat = None
  1138	            if not rec["defects"] and not rec["terminal_gate"] and not LATCH:
  1139	                unsat = dict(rec, status="UNSAT", count_for_scientific_coverage=True,
  1140	                             commit="UNSAT chosen at an explicit commit boundary with no earlier cancel stamp or latch")
  1141	            commit = commit_record(rec_path, dict(rec, commit="UNKNOWN record"), unsat)
  1142	            rec["status"] = "UNSAT" if commit["committed_unsat"] else "UNKNOWN"
  1143	            summary.setdefault("commits", {})[it["id"]] = commit
  1144	        else:
  1145	            rec["reason"] = "no settled self-exit rc20 verdict with a complete proof sink (or latch/cancel)"
  1146	            rec["host_clock_end"] = clock()
  1147	            write_x(rec_path, rec)
  1148	        summary["items"][it["id"]] = {k: rec.get(k) for k in ("status", "defects", "terminal_gate", "reason", "proof")}
  1149	        STATE["remaining_reserve"] -= ITEM_RESERVE
  1150	        if LATCH or CANCEL.is_set():
  1151	            summary["stopped"] = {"phase": "latch_or_cancel", "item": it["id"]}
  1152	            return
  1153	    outcome["complete"] = True
  1154	
  1155	
  1156	def final_code(summary, outcome):
  1157	    """Computed exit code (never inferred from a plain return) and the computed settlement summary."""
  1158	    out_dir = STATE["out"]
  1159	    if outcome["sat"]:
  1160	        code = EXIT_SAT_CANDIDATE
  1161	    elif LATCH:
  1162	        code = EXIT_SAFETY_STOP
  1163	    elif outcome["exception"]:
  1164	        code = EXIT_EXCEPTION
  1165	    elif CANCEL.is_set():
  1166	        code = EXIT_CANCELLED
  1167	    elif outcome["controls_failed"]:
  1168	        code = EXIT_CONTROLS_FAILED
  1169	    elif outcome["refused"]:
  1170	        code = EXIT_ADMISSION_REFUSED
  1171	    elif outcome["complete"]:
  1172	        code = EXIT_PLAN_COMPLETE
  1173	    else:
  1174	        code = EXIT_EXCEPTION
  1175	    children = [c.settled_rec if c.settled_rec is not None else {"seq": c.seq, "label": c.label, "pid": c.pid,
  1176	                                                                   "settled": False, "note": "never settled"}
  1177	                for c in OWNED]
  1178	    all_settled = all(r.get("settled") for r in children)
  1179	    if not all_settled and code != EXIT_SAT_CANDIDATE:
  1180	        code = EXIT_SAFETY_STOP
  1181	    summary.update(exit_code=code, latch=list(LATCH), cancel=CANCEL.is_set(), cancel_order=STATE["cancel_order"],
  1182	                   children=[{k: r.get(k) for k in ("seq", "label", "pid", "returncode", "stop", "settled")}
  1183	                             for r in children],
  1184	                   all_children_settled=all_settled)
  1185	    try:
  1186	        summary["host_clock_end"] = clock()
  1187	        write_x(os.path.join(out_dir, "summary.json"), summary)
  1188	    except BaseException as exc:
  1189	        print("SUMMARY WRITE FAILED %r" % exc, flush=True)
  1190	        code = EXIT_SAFETY_STOP
  1191	    print("SUMMARY", os.path.join(out_dir, "summary.json"), "exit", code, flush=True)
  1192	    return code
  1193	
  1194	
  1195	if __name__ == "__main__":
  1196	    sys.exit(main())

    11	
    12	ROOT = Path('/Users/jinleic/repos/aifours')
    13	RUN = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
    14	stopped = False
    15	
    16	
    17	def stop(signum, frame):
    18	    global stopped
    19	    stopped = True
    20	
    21	
    22	def clock():
    23	    observed = subprocess.check_output(['/bin/date', '+%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z'], text=True).strip()
    24	    return datetime.strptime(observed, '%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z').isoformat(timespec='seconds')
    25	
    26	
    27	def observe():
    28	    stat = os.statvfs(ROOT)
    29	    return {'t': clock(), 'free': stat.f_bavail * stat.f_frsize,
    30	            'floor_bytes': 12000000000, 'scope': 'metadata only; not scientific checkpoint or launch admission'}
    31	
    32	
    33	def main():
    34	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    35	    parser.add_argument('--once', action='store_true')
    36	    parser.add_argument('--output', required=True)
    37	    args = parser.parse_args()
    38	    out = Path(args.output).resolve()
    39	    if not out.is_relative_to(RUN):
    40	        raise SystemExit('Output must be inside the current run')
    41	    contract = json.loads((RUN / 'contract-v1.json').read_text())
    42	    end = datetime.fromisoformat(contract['cutoffs']['owned_compute_settled_by']).timestamp()
    43	    for sig in (signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGINT):
    44	        signal.signal(sig, stop)
    45	    with out.open('x') as handle:
    46	        start = {'event': 'observer_start', 'at': clock(), 'pid': os.getpid(), 'pgid': os.getpgrp(),
    47	                 'cwd': str(Path.cwd()), 'source_sha256': hashlib.sha256(Path(__file__).read_bytes()).hexdigest()}
    48	        handle.write(json.dumps(start) + '\n')
    49	        while not stopped and time.time() < end:
    50	            record = observe()
    51	            handle.write(json.dumps(record) + '\n')
    52	            handle.flush()
    53	            print(json.dumps(record), flush=True)
    54	            if args.once:
    55	                break
    56	            target = min(time.monotonic() + 10, time.monotonic() + max(0, end - time.time()))
    57	            while not stopped and time.monotonic() < target:
    58	                time.sleep(min(1, max(0, target - time.monotonic())))
    59	        handle.write(json.dumps({'event': 'observer_end', 'at': clock(), 'sigterm_or_sigint': stopped}) + '\n')
    60	        handle.flush()
    61	    return 0

   781	+    the boundary is one atomic next(ORDER); a cancel stamped before it forces UNKNOWN."""
   782	+    unknown_bytes = json.dumps(rec_unknown, indent=1, sort_keys=True).encode()
   783	+    unsat_bytes = None if rec_unsat is None else json.dumps(rec_unsat, indent=1, sort_keys=True).encode()
   784	+    fd = os.open(path, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL, 0o644)
   785	+    try:
   786	+        boundary = next(ORDER)
   787	+        co = STATE["cancel_order"]
   788	+        chosen = unsat_bytes if (unsat_bytes is not None and not LATCH and (co is None or co > boundary)) else unknown_bytes
   789	+        view = memoryview(chosen)
   790	+        while view:
   791	+            k = os.write(fd, view)
   792	+            view = view[k:]
   793	+        os.fsync(fd)
   794	+    finally:
   795	+        os.close(fd)
   796	+    return {"boundary": boundary, "cancel_order": STATE["cancel_order"], "committed_unsat": chosen is unsat_bytes,
   797	+            "sha256": hashlib.sha256(chosen).hexdigest()}

   977	unsigned int getHash (int* input) {
   978	  unsigned int sum = 0, prod = 1, xor = 0;
   979	  while (*input) {
   980	    prod *= *input; sum += *input; xor ^= *input; input++; }
   981	  return (1023 * sum + prod ^ (31 * xor)) % BIGINIT; }
   982	
   983	int read_lit (struct solver *S, int *lit) {
   984	  int l = 0, lc, shift = 0;
   985	  do {
   986	    lc = getc_unlocked (S->proofFile);
   987	    S->nReads++;
   988	    if ((shift == 0) && (lc == EOF)) return EOF;
   989	    l |= (lc & 127) << shift;
   990	    shift += 7; }
   991	  while (lc > 127);
   992	  if (l % 2) *lit = (l >> 1) * -1;
   993	  else       *lit = (l >> 1);
   994	  return 1; }

The in-memory checks confirmed two failures: emergency cleanup can escape without reaping or retaining the child, and cancellation during final closeout can still produce exit 0 with `cancel=false`. They also confirmed that `on_term()` holds a non-reentrant Event lock while running Python code, so a nested signal handler can deadlock it. I’m finishing the control-health and budget checks.

codex
A simulated full-plan run confirmed that a checker’s `MEMOUT` diagnostic does not latch a stop: the runner launches the next item and can finish with exit 0. C2 also accepts MEMOUT and TIMEOUT exits as healthy. The proof, log, and CNF reservation arithmetic adds up; the remaining issues are the unbounded JSON allowance and a 120-second settlement budget covering up to 190 seconds of serial waits.
